# Raphael product rules


## A failure is information: a member keeps going until no plausible way is left (owner, 2026-11-14)

Owner: members give up too fast. Like Hermes Agent, a member treats a failed call as information, takes the next plausible way, and asks the owner only for what only the owner can give, or when the ways left cost far more than the result is worth. Cause, from a language-teaching member's turn (told to use its own computer for a news page, it read the lead, had its page refused twice, ended with 「我不會再換方式繞過這個攔截」 and never opened its browser): every word of the harness on that path said stop. These rows change the harness for every member and every brain; no member's own routines, skills, instructions or memory are edited.

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-PERSIST-01 | 2026-11-14 | No prompt caps persistence. Gone: "Diagnosis: inspect once, change approach once, then report the exact blocker" (the about index every prompt reads), "change approach at most once before reporting the blocker" (the shared doctrine), "use a different approach at most once" (the corrective note), "Do not try another command or path" (the data floor's refusal), "report it plainly after one check" (the guardrails' note) and the worker's own "When a tool fails, try another way" (said once now, for all). One rule stands in their place, in the shared contract that every conversation (API, narrated, CLI; chief and members) and every task reads: a failed call is information; read its cause, fix what you can, then take the next plausible way (another tool, other arguments, another source, your computer's browser when you have one); ask the owner only for what only they can give, or when the ways left cost far more than the result is worth; never claim that a failed call succeeded. A part answer looks for the rest first, then names a gap left once (R-WORK-17). The default prompt stays inside its budget (the default prompt test: before 15939 / 15204 tokens for an API / CLI brain, after 15929 / 15206, limits 16048 / 15212). | pytest | hard | core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_every_prompt_carries_the_one_rule_and_none_of_the_caps; core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_the_rule_is_said_once_in_the_shared_contract_and_the_diagnosis_cap_is_gone; core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_a_part_answer_looks_for_the_rest_first; core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_the_data_floor_refusal_points_at_the_right_tools_and_no_longer_forbids_every_other_way; core/tests/agent/test_member_behaviour.py::test_a_gap_is_named_once_and_only_when_it_matters_for_every_speaker; core/tests/members/test_management_discovery.py::test_default_production_prompt_stays_below_the_measured_pre_unit_budget |
| R-PERSIST-02 | 2026-11-14 | A failed call is told as "refused" only when the owner or a safety rule decided it (an owner's no, a declined or cancelled call, a safety rule, presence required, a private-only tool, the member limit, the data floor, a connection the owner turned off). A call the harness held back (no answer in time, a paused batch, a ceiling, a pending connection, the desktop in use) is "not made". Any other failure (a URL to look up first, a page check, a sleeping computer, a site's error page, a stopped Docker) is "failed", with the harness's fixed words of what to do instead (never an internal code beside them), or else the tool's own kind and its own words held to 2000 characters (Hermes bounds an error at 2048) and still redacted, and "Another way may still work." The same words reach an API, a background and a CLI brain (the CLI's MCP results no longer fold a cause into a vague kind). A web read that a site turned away (401 or 403, an error page) says that is no sign-in of the owner's and that a browser or another source may still work. | pytest | hard | core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_only_the_owner_and_the_safety_rules_refuse; core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_any_other_failure_is_a_failure_with_its_cause_and_another_way; core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_a_tools_own_words_of_why_are_held_to_a_bound_and_stay_redacted; core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_a_call_the_harness_held_back_is_not_made_and_is_no_way_that_failed; core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_a_web_read_turned_away_says_it_is_no_sign_in_of_the_owners; core/tests/worker/test_context.py::test_a_refused_call_is_never_read_as_done |
| R-PERSIST-03 | 2026-11-14 | A turn about to end right after a failed call is told, before it may, what failed in it ("Failed: show_page (secret).") and which of the tools it was offered it has not called ("Offered and not tried yet: ..."), and to take the next plausible way or say why none fits: once for each round of failed calls, a reply to it that makes no call ending the turn; at most once in a conversation turn and twice in a background run (nobody is there to say "try again"). A failure the harness told in words is listed by its tool alone. A CLI brain makes its own model calls, so it reads the same list with the result of each tool's first failure in the turn (three times a turn at most). Never after an owner's no, never for a call held back, and never a count of how many ways to try. | pytest | hard | core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_a_turn_that_gives_up_after_a_failed_call_is_told_what_failed_and_what_it_has_not_tried; core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_the_note_never_follows_an_owners_no_or_a_call_held_back; core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_a_background_run_is_told_twice_and_an_interactive_turn_once; core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_a_cli_brain_reads_the_same_list_with_the_result_of_a_tools_first_failure; core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_a_cli_brain_is_not_pushed_after_the_owner_said_no; core/tests/agent/test_self_diagnosis.py::test_api_corrective_round_is_bounded_and_never_follows_an_owner_denial |
| R-PERSIST-04 | 2026-11-14 | A short reply that ends on a step it announces with a verb of looking or doing (「我先去看看…」, 「先查一下…」, 「接著打開…」, 「まず…します」, "I'll first check …", "Let me check …") is asked to go on, like "let me now" was; one that only mentions a step ("Let me know …", 「我先說結論…」, 「請先確認…」) is an answer. Calls of more than one tool that fail one after another get, every third, the note "Do not switch to text-only replies; keep using tools, but diagnose first", which no one tool's count gave; it never ends a task. | pytest | hard | core/tests/worker/test_persistence_guards.py::test_a_reply_that_ends_on_a_step_it_announces_is_asked_to_go_on; core/tests/worker/test_persistence_guards.py::test_an_answer_that_only_mentions_a_step_is_not_taken_for_an_intent; core/tests/worker/test_persistence_guards.py::test_failures_of_different_tools_one_after_another_get_the_keep_using_tools_note; core/tests/worker/test_persistence_guards.py::test_a_success_between_failures_starts_the_count_again; core/tests/worker/test_persistence_guards.py::test_one_tool_failing_keeps_its_own_note_and_gets_no_second; core/tests/worker/test_persistence_guards.py::test_the_note_after_repeated_failures_never_says_to_report_after_one_check |
| R-PERSIST-05 | 2026-11-14 | Yesterday's failure is no rule for today. The conversation summary keeps a failure only under "Blocked:", dated, with the tool or source and the exact cause, dropped once a later turn got past it, and never as a rule, a decision or a capability ("X does not work", "cannot get the news", "ask the user to paste it instead"); "Recent decisions:" holds only what the owner decided. Every brain reads the summary as background, not instructions, "what failed then may work now, and your tools remain fully active" (Hermes's clause). An answered turn whose short reply (800 characters at most) says it could not go on, and whose last call failed, is read back by later turns, tasks and folds as a request that did not finish, naming the calls that failed and that other ways were not ruled out, never as its "I cannot"; a turn with a call the owner or a safety rule said no to is read as it was. Harness words only: nothing stored is rewritten. | pytest | hard | core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_an_answer_that_gave_up_after_a_failed_call_is_read_back_as_unfinished; core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_an_answer_that_got_past_a_failure_or_delivered_its_work_is_read_as_it_was; core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_an_answer_after_the_owners_no_stays_as_it_was; core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_the_summary_keeps_failures_as_dated_blockers_never_as_rules; core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_every_brain_reads_the_summary_as_background_with_its_tools_active |
| R-PERSIST-06 | 2026-11-14 | A page the chosen reader could not read as a whole (timeout, unreachable, an error page, a refused or spent key, nothing a page is made of) is read once more by the next reader the owner set up: the built-in reader after a service, and after the built-in reader the first reading service whose key the owner stored; never a service he did not set up, and the next read goes to his choice again. The result says which reader read it (`reader_fallback`, the step's service). A service changes, never the model. When no reader could, the member's own browser reads it (as for a thin page) on more than one kind of failure: an error page, a 401 or 403, a timeout, a failed connection, nothing a page is made of; a wrong, forbidden or too large address never goes there. A consent or terms step in front of a page asks for the browser by its button words anywhere (「内容について確認しました」, "agree and continue") and by its title (「ご利用にあたって」, "Terms of use") on a page short enough to be little more than it; a whole article with a terms link in its footer does not. | pytest | hard | core/tests/services/test_read_fallback.py::test_the_built_in_reader_that_fails_is_followed_once_by_the_reader_the_owner_has_a_key_for; core/tests/services/test_read_fallback.py::test_with_no_other_reader_set_up_the_url_goes_to_no_service_and_the_first_failure_stands; core/tests/services/test_read_fallback.py::test_a_reading_service_that_fails_is_followed_by_the_built_in_reader; core/tests/services/test_read_fallback.py::test_when_both_fail_the_first_readers_failure_is_what_is_told; core/tests/services/test_read_fallback.py::test_an_address_that_is_wrong_forbidden_or_too_big_is_not_sent_to_another_reader; core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_a_page_that_turned_the_reader_away_is_read_in_the_members_browser; core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_an_address_that_is_wrong_or_forbidden_never_goes_to_the_browser; core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_a_consent_or_terms_step_in_front_of_a_page_asks_for_a_browser_though_its_navigation_is_long |

A member with every connection is told it is not limited. | pytest | hard | core/tests/agent/test_persistence.py::test_self_check_tells_a_limited_member_which_apps_it_may_use_and_who_changes_that |

## The interface language is the window's alone (owner, 2026-11-14)

Owner, 2026-11-14: 「我不是說過系統的 UI 語言並不會影響到使用者內部聊天的語言嗎？它的 UI 語言就是單純 UI 語言而已，它也不會去到 system prompt，什麼事都不會發生」. This replaces the configured reply language every external turn and every background task was told (`reply_context`, 「Owner's configured reply language: …」) and the interface locale every system prompt named (`interface_context`, 「Interface locale: … This selects fixed UI labels only.」), and supersedes R-AG-LANG-01's "interface metadata" in features/conversation.md.

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-LANG-01 | 2026-11-14 | The interface language (device.json `ui_language`, the `ui_language` of `agent.ask`, `rooms.send`, `members.create`) reaches no model. No system prompt names it (Raphael's, a member's, a CLI brain's, a task's, a member's task, a reaction's, a greeting's, a computer run's: none of the builders takes a language); no message to a model carries it (the configured reply language line that went with Telegram, Slack, Discord, connector and colleague turns and with every background task, routines included, is gone); no reply schema offers its prefixes (the model names one of five fixed kinds, `REPLY_KINDS`: answer, notice, negative, understood, question); no tool result is in it (an approval nobody answered tells the model the fixed `EXPIRED_NOTE`, never the interface's phrase). With the interface in Japanese and the owner writing Traditional Chinese, everything a model is sent is the same as on any other interface. | pytest | hard | core/tests/agent/test_conversation_language.py::test_no_prompt_builder_takes_a_language; core/tests/agent/test_conversation_language.py::test_the_interface_language_has_no_words_for_a_model_left; core/tests/agent/test_conversation_language.py::test_the_reply_kinds_are_one_fixed_set_and_read_as_the_windows_prefix; core/tests/agent/test_conversation_language.py::test_a_reply_to_chinese_words_on_a_japanese_interface_names_no_language; core/tests/members/test_interface_language.py::test_a_member_is_told_nothing_of_a_japanese_interface_when_the_owner_writes_chinese; core/tests/members/test_interface_language.py::test_a_messaging_chat_on_a_japanese_interface_is_not_told_to_reply_in_japanese; core/tests/background/test_interface_language.py::test_a_routine_written_in_chinese_is_told_nothing_of_a_japanese_interface; core/tests/agent/test_owner_chat_language.py::test_an_external_turn_is_told_no_interface_language; core/tests/agent/test_ui_languages.py::test_every_locale_has_fixed_copy_and_no_brain_path_names_it; core/tests/computer/test_run.py::test_the_prompt_asks_for_the_owners_language_and_names_no_interface_language; core/tests/agent/test_cli_confirmation.py::test_an_expired_confirmation_declines_only_its_call; core/tests/agent/test_cli_confirmation.py::test_an_expired_claude_permission_names_the_unanswered_action |
| R-LANG-02 | 2026-11-14 | A member replies in the language the owner writes to it in: the model reads the owner's words, and the shared rule (`REPLY_LANGUAGE`) says its own persona and the owner's explicit request come first, then the owner's words or the conversation. A member whose own instructions set a language (a Japanese teacher who always answers in Japanese) keeps it on every interface. A task or a routine with no message of the owner's is told with the task, never in the system prompt (R-CACHE-02), to write in the language the owner's own messages show, else the language of the task's own text (`TASK_LANGUAGE`, which names no language). A new member's greeting, with no word of the owner's yet, is asked for in the language its own instructions set, else the language of the owner's profile and its memory of them, else that of its own name, role and instructions (`GREETING_NOW`, in the turn's message). | pytest | hard | core/tests/members/test_interface_language.py::test_a_japanese_teachers_own_instructions_still_decide_her_language; core/tests/agent/test_conversation_language.py::test_a_greeting_and_a_task_are_told_whose_language_without_naming_one; core/tests/background/test_interface_language.py::test_a_routine_written_in_chinese_is_told_nothing_of_a_japanese_interface; core/tests/members/test_management_discovery.py::test_default_production_prompt_stays_below_the_measured_pre_unit_budget |

## Connect Apps: one button per row, one way back, one mark, a search that finds the name (owner, 2026-11-14)

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-UI-154 | 2026-11-14 | Owner, with a picture of the connected list, after asking more than three times: 「左上角的 connected 返回鍵也是可以按的」. The connected list's chevron and its title are one button that goes back; there is no separate chevron beside the title. | vitest, look | hard | app/src/main/connections.test.ts::are one list behind the chip, with a back chevron: a row for each service with its mark, name, line and switch; connect-apps-back-title; connect-apps-back-title-press |

A row of Connect Apps (the main view and the connected list) is one button: one hover fill over the whole row, 8 px clear of its mark, with 連結, 「✓ 已連結」 or the switch standing on it; the list gives the fill room on both sides so its corners stay round and are never cut square. | vitest, look | hard | app/src/main/connections.test.ts::a row is one button: the pointer and one hover fill over the whole row, with 連結 or its switch on it, in the connected list and the main view alike; connect-apps-row-hover; connect-apps-connected-rows; connect-apps-connected-hover |

Found apps are ordered by how well their own names answer the query: the app whose name (or id, or other name) is the query, then names that start with it, names with a word that starts with it, names that hold it, ids and other names that hold it, and last the apps only their descriptions find. | vitest | hard | app/src/main/connections.test.ts::the app whose name is what was typed comes first, then names that start with it or hold it, then the rest |

An app's mark comes from one renderer (`entryIcon`) everywhere it is named: the list, the main view, the @ menu, a card. ## A member does what its owner asks, in full, and says plainly what it did not do (owner, 2026-11-14)

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-WORK-16 | 2026-11-14 | Owner: a language-teaching member, asked for daily reading pages from real news for his own study with the source credited, lectured on copyright in five replies and then delivered a page of only a headline and one sentence, though he had said it was for his own use and asked for everything written in full at his level with the source's link. Cause: nothing in Raphael's prompts speaks of copyright, so the refusal was the model's own; what the prompts lacked was that this is the owner's own private app and his own use, and what they had pushed the other way (a closed page was to be replaced by "another source … rewritten simply for a learner"). A member's prompt (reply, CLI conversation, task, routine; the chief's task too) now says: what the owner asks for his own use is made in full, from the source read: the complete rewrite at his level, every vocabulary and grammar note, the translations, the source's link inside the page; a headline, a lead or a summary is never the delivery; once he has said what it is for, no refusal, lecture or repeated reservation; if the model's own policy really stops it copying a text word for word, it says so once in one short sentence and delivers the complete rewritten material anyway. The chief's conversation prompt keeps its size budget (the default prompt test, 15909 of 16048 tokens for an API brain and 15174 of 15212 for a CLI one) and does not carry the block. | pytest | hard | core/tests/agent/test_member_behaviour.py::test_every_member_prompt_says_to_do_what_the_owner_asks_in_full_in_his_own_app; core/tests/agent/test_member_behaviour.py::test_the_chiefs_conversation_prompt_is_not_given_the_members_block; core/tests/members/test_management_discovery.py::test_default_production_prompt_stays_below_the_measured_pre_unit_budget |
| R-WORK-17 | 2026-11-14 | Same conversation: nearly every reply ended with a line saying the member would not call the page complete, and a routine's report listed what it had not checked, repeated after the owner had understood. Cause in the prompts: a report was to include "checks and anything unfinished" (task, routine and CLI reports), a part-answer was to "name the gap" with no limit, and a closed page was a gap to name. Honesty stays (never invent a fact; only claim what a tool result established), the self-protective boilerplate goes: what was not done is said once, plainly, at the end, and only when it matters; no repeated disclaimers and no lines that announce what the member will not pretend. | pytest | hard | core/tests/agent/test_member_behaviour.py::test_every_member_prompt_is_honest_without_hedging; core/tests/agent/test_member_behaviour.py::test_the_words_that_taught_the_boilerplate_are_gone; core/tests/agent/test_member_behaviour.py::test_a_gap_is_named_once_and_only_when_it_matters_for_every_speaker; core/tests/agent/test_member_behaviour.py::test_a_task_reports_what_it_made_by_its_title_and_what_is_not_done_once |
| R-WORK-18 | 2026-11-14 | Owner, when the reader met a consent page, told the member three times to read it in its own computer, and that he allowed it: the member agreed three times and did not open the page. A blocked read (a consent or terms page, a timeout, text that stops short) is tried in the member's own computer's browser first, when the member has the computer tool, before another source or giving up; a consent step the owner told it to take is already his yes; what it can do now it does in this turn (a promise to do it later is not an answer). A member with no computer tool says once that it has none and that the owner can turn it on in its Computer tab, and never claims an ability it lacks. Waking the computer stays the member's own tool call (R-COMP-WAKE-01). | pytest | hard | core/tests/agent/test_member_behaviour.py::test_every_member_prompt_says_to_use_its_own_computer_before_giving_up_on_a_page; core/tests/agent/test_member_behaviour.py::test_a_task_opens_the_page_in_its_own_browser_before_looking_for_another_source |
| R-WORK-19 | 2026-11-14 | Owner's check: Raphael asked the member for the title of the reading page it had made at 07:00 and was told that the record did not hold it. A member's later turns, in a conversation, a task or a routine, read each page it made as a card in the turns they read: its title, its page_id, its revision, when it was made (and changed), and for a page of its own (`html`, a report) the start of its visible text (360 characters, the plain rendition of a report, never raw html, scripts or styles; a plugin's page stays out). The text is untrusted data like every card outcome. There is no tool that reads a whole page back: a member that must change one uses update_page with the id. | pytest | hard | core/tests/agent/test_member_behaviour.py::test_a_later_turn_reads_the_title_the_date_and_the_start_of_the_text_of_a_page_the_member_made; core/tests/agent/test_member_behaviour.py::test_the_prompt_of_the_members_next_task_names_the_page_it_made_that_morning; core/tests/agent/test_member_behaviour.py::test_a_plugins_page_and_raw_html_never_enter_the_context; core/tests/agent/test_card_context.py::test_every_window_card_has_a_safe_projection_and_owner_answers_are_untrusted |

## Memory, attachments and team reads are not locked against the owner (owner, 2026-11-14)

Owner: 「我覺得現在的機密資訊鎖得太死了，理論上根本不用鎖這麼死…除非是什麼密碼啊、金鑰啊，不然是什麼 credit cards 的密碼什麼的，那個才是機密啊」. What stays closed is a credential; what the owner asked for, or gave a member himself, is not.

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-MEM-12 | 2026-11-14 | Owner, 2026-11-14: he corrected a street name in his address to a member, `memory_update` was refused as `unconfirmed_only` five times (turns 1290 to 1382) and the memory kept the typo. A turn that read outside content (a page, a search, a colleague) still marks what it writes and still never changes an entry the owner confirmed, but a `replace` the owner asked for in his own words of that private turn goes through: every span it writes (and every span it only takes out) stands in the owner's message, with up to two neighbouring characters, so that words he never said (a page's instruction, a rewrite beyond the correction) never pass, and a removal is no correction. The entry stays the owner's (no mark added); an outside claim he corrects stays marked. A room, a colleague's work and a routine have no owner words, so the guard is unchanged there (R-MEM-03, R-MEM-07). | pytest | hard | core/tests/memory/test_files.py::test_the_owners_own_correction_in_a_turn_that_read_outside_content_replaces_a_confirmed_entry; core/tests/memory/test_files.py::test_an_owner_correction_never_confirms_the_outside_claim_it_edits_or_lets_other_words_in; core/tests/memory/test_files.py::test_outside_content_enters_marked_and_never_changes_what_the_owner_said |
| R-MEM-13 | 2026-11-14 | Owner, 2026-11-14: he corrected a fact a member had wrong (turn 692), and its own instructions still stated the old fact three days later (turn 812): memory edits never reach the member's instructions. The doctrine every member reads (one sentence, beside the skill line) tells it to propose the fix of its own instructions with `member_update` on its own id when the owner corrects a fact they state: the owner's yes decides, like any change to a member, and nothing edits his data directly. | pytest | hard | core/tests/agent/test_skill_guidance.py::test_the_persona_carries_the_skill_line_beside_the_memory_doctrine; default prompt budget (`verify --contract`) |
| R-FLOOR-03 | 2026-11-14 | Owner, 2026-11-14: a member's local commands to read its own attachments (the files the owner attached in a conversation, stored in the app's `attachments` folder under their opaque ids) were refused as `protected_data` five times, because a read with a brace or a parenthesis (`if (Test-Path ...) { Get-Content ... }`) was taken for a write. A command or file tool that only reads such a file is no access to the app's data at all; one that changes it (a redirect, `Set-Content`, `Remove-Item`, a program that could) is still refused as before. The attachment's sidecar, every other file of the data folder (the database, the vault, keys, settings, `.env`), a sub-folder and the folder itself stay protected, and a command that names one of them beside an attachment is judged as before (R-FLOOR-01, R-FLOOR-02). | pytest | hard | core/tests/agent/test_data_floor.py::test_a_member_reads_the_files_the_owner_attached_in_its_conversation; core/tests/agent/test_data_floor.py::test_an_attachment_is_still_not_written_by_a_command; core/tests/agent/test_data_floor.py::test_credentials_and_the_rest_of_the_data_folder_stay_protected_beside_attachments |
| R-ROUTINE-09 | 2026-11-14 | Owner, 2026-11-14: a team health check of a routine raised an approval card for every member it read (16 on 11-10 and 11-11, two waited ten minutes), and the overnight runs were cancelled (turns 1287, 1301). In a routine's run, `member_manage` actions that only read (listing a member's routines, skills or commands, reading its settings: `READS`) ask for no card and are T1, not T3; every change keeps its tier and its exact-action card, and a destructive one its T3. | pytest | hard | core/tests/members/test_routine_reads.py::test_a_routines_run_reads_any_member_with_no_card_and_a_change_still_asks |

## Each day of the all-day band opens by itself (owner, 2026-11-14)

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-CAL-08 | 2026-11-14 | Owner, with a picture of the week view whose all-day band showed 「還有 N 個」 under several days (pressing one opened the band of every day, and one 收合 folded them all). Each day opens by itself: pressing a day's 「還有 N 個」 opens only that day's column to every all-day item of that day, with that day's own 「收合」 under them (a real button with aria-expanded and aria-controls, like R-CAL-07); every other day stays folded to three rows with its own 「還有 N 個」; several days may be open at once; 「收合」 folds only its own day. The band is as tall as its tallest column, and the hours move by that growth without resizing, as R-CAL-07. A bar that crosses an open and a folded day is never lost and never overlaps: it stays whole across the days that show it (any bar in the first three rows) and, below the fold, is drawn in one piece for each run of open days, cut where a folded day hides it (a cut side is square, a cut end has no resize handle) and settled into the first free row of its open days; a day's count is still exactly the items it does not draw. Keyboard: after opening, focus stands on that day's 「收合」; after folding, on that day's 「還有 N 個」. The choice lasts while the calendar shows that day or week, and any other range, or coming back to it, finds every day folded. ## A file the owner attaches reaches the model as it is (owner, 2026-11-14)

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-ATTACH-RAW-01 | 2026-11-14 | Owner, after attaching an `.html` file so that a member (a Japanese teacher) could copy its design, and being told it only receives the page's visible text, with `<style>` and `<script>` dropped, so that sending it again changed nothing: 「傳送檔案不是會傳送原始檔嗎? 應該要是 raw data 才對」. A file the owner attaches is the owner's own data. A text file (html, htm, css, js, ts, svg, xml, json, md, csv, code and any other text type the reader knows) reaches every speaker, API or CLI brain, a private conversation, a room and the background worker alike, as its source text, character for character: nothing is stripped, turned into "readable" text or JSON-escaped (the text follows a line of facts, not inside a JSON string). Root cause: `file_reader.read_text` ran every `.html` through a visible-text extractor (`ReadableHTML`) before the prompt was built, and the inline context then wrapped the result in JSON. The extractor is gone; a readable rendition beside the source was not added, because the source already holds the words and the rendition would spend the same budget twice. The untrusted-data wrapper stays: the prompt's own delimiter tags inside the file are neutralized, and its text is data, never instructions. A CLI brain also gets the stored original (`Files:` path), byte for byte. Binary formats keep their handling (images as images, PDF and Office text extraction, media transcription). | pytest | hard | core/tests/agent/test_raw_attachments.py::test_an_api_brain_receives_the_html_with_its_style_and_script; core/tests/agent/test_raw_attachments.py::test_a_cli_brain_receives_the_html_source_and_the_original_file; core/tests/agent/test_raw_attachments.py::test_every_text_type_reaches_the_model_as_its_source_not_escaped_or_extracted; core/tests/agent/test_raw_attachments.py::test_the_reader_and_the_read_attachment_tool_give_the_same_source; core/tests/agent/test_raw_attachments.py::test_raw_source_cannot_close_the_wrapper_or_speak_as_the_owner; core/tests/agent/test_raw_attachments.py::test_binary_formats_keep_their_handling; core/tests/background/test_attachment_text.py::test_room_html_reaches_the_speaker_without_a_tool_call; core/tests/background/test_attachment_text.py::test_worker_prompt_gives_an_html_file_as_its_source_and_says_where_a_long_one_is_stored; core/tests/agent/test_attachment_text.py::test_html_is_read_as_its_source_not_as_a_readable_page; core/tests/services/test_file_reader.py::test_text_readers |
| R-ATTACH-RAW-02 | 2026-11-14 | A text file longer than the message's limit (24,000 characters a file, 40,000 for all files) is not pasted. Owner, 2026-11-14: a member given a 136 KB `.html` page to copy saw only its first 15 KB, and all of that was `@font-face { src: url(data:font/woff2;base64,...) }`; it asked to read on from a given position and the owner wanted it to read the whole file without editing it. The file reaches the model the way a file reaches Claude Code: a line of facts (name, type, size in bytes, `lines`, `pasted: false`), a note that names the tool (`read_attachment (index N, offset 1)`) and where the complete original is stored on this computer, so that an API, ChatGPT sign-in or CLI brain alike reads it by number or opens the path with its own file tool. A file that fits is still pasted whole, as written, as before (R-ATTACH-RAW-01); a file that does not fit has no start pasted, because a start of 15 KB is the part least likely to hold the words. | pytest | hard | core/tests/agent/test_raw_attachments.py::test_a_file_over_the_limit_is_a_file_to_read_with_its_facts_and_where_it_is; core/tests/agent/test_attachment_read_on.py::test_a_file_that_fits_the_message_is_still_pasted_as_written; core/tests/agent/test_attachment_read_on.py::test_a_long_file_is_never_pasted_but_named_with_what_a_member_needs_to_read_it; core/tests/agent/test_attachment_text.py::test_each_inline_file_and_the_combined_message_have_separate_budgets; core/tests/agent/test_attachment_text.py::test_attached_instructions_cannot_close_the_untrusted_wrapper_and_size_is_bounded; core/tests/agent/test_attachment_text.py::test_a_chip_only_pasted_article_reaches_the_member_and_the_real_attachment_reader; core/tests/background/test_attachment_text.py::test_worker_prompt_gives_an_html_file_as_its_source_and_says_where_a_long_one_is_stored |
| R-ATTACH-RAW-03 | 2026-11-14 | `read_attachment` reads a text file as Claude Code's Read tool does (owner, 2026-11-14: follow Claude Code's own design for reading files, for every brain). It takes `offset` (the 1-based line to start from) and `limit` (how many lines), gives up to 2000 lines by default, each numbered like `cat -n` (a right-aligned number, a tab, the line; the number is no part of the line), cuts a line longer than 2000 characters with `… [N more characters]` and a note, and when there is more says so with `next_offset`, `end_reached: false` and a note that names the next call (`read_attachment (index N, offset M)`); one call also stops at 60,000 characters so that long lines never fill the window. Base64 data inside a line over 2000 characters is first shown as a short marker (`[98 KB of base64 data elided]`, counted in `elided_characters`), so that a font `data:` URI in a minified file does not hide the style, markup and script after it; the stored file is never changed. Reading the owner's own attachments needs no approval (the tool is T0 and the stored file stays readable, R-FLOOR-03). | pytest | hard | core/tests/agent/test_attachment_read_on.py::test_a_design_file_with_an_embedded_font_is_read_whole_with_its_style_markup_and_script; core/tests/agent/test_attachment_read_on.py::test_a_line_over_2000_characters_is_cut_with_a_note_of_what_was_left; core/tests/agent/test_attachment_read_on.py::test_it_says_when_there_is_more_and_from_which_line_and_each_page_joins_the_next; core/tests/agent/test_attachment_read_on.py::test_a_page_of_very_long_lines_stops_at_the_character_cap_and_goes_on_from_there; core/tests/agent/test_attachment_read_on.py::test_the_edges_of_a_file; core/tests/agent/test_attachment_read_on.py::test_the_tool_arguments_are_checked; core/tests/agent/test_attachment_read_on.py::test_the_read_attachment_tool_reads_the_owners_file_on_and_never_changes_it; core/tests/agent/test_attachment_read_on.py::test_a_member_reads_on_with_offset_and_limit_through_the_tool_call; core/tests/agent/test_raw_attachments.py::test_the_reader_and_the_read_attachment_tool_give_the_same_source |


## The card of changed files says what happened (owner, 2026-11-14)

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-CARD-15 | 2026-11-14 | Owner, with a picture of a card titled 「工作結果」 with a green 「● 完成」 and 「53 個檔案變更 +0 −0」 (「你可以看一下 grokbot 是怎麼做的，因為我不記得他們會有那個完成的點點。然後這個工作結果我也不知道是在幹嘛」): the card under a finished turn that changed files on the member's computer says in its title what happened, in plain words and the number of files: 「改了 N 個檔案」, 「新增了 N 個檔案」 when every file is new, 「刪除了 N 個檔案」 when every file is gone, 「已復原 N 個檔案的變更」 once undone. It is the second exception, after the owner question (R-CARD-08), to R-CARD-01's textual status. Below the title is a well of rows, one per file, as the question card's rows: the file's name, its folder in the quiet colour (團隊資料夾 or 工作資料夾, then the path) and at the right what happened to it. Five rows show; any others fold under one line 「還有 N 個檔案」. A file with text to read opens under its row; one with none is only named. A row says 「新增」 for a file that came, 「刪除」 for one that went, the lines (`+12 −3`, a side that is zero left out) for a text file that changed, and 「已修改」 for a changed file with no lines to count (a picture, a large file, a mode change). ## The first call after a wake is waited for; "your computer" is the member's (owner, 2026-11-14)

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-COMP-FIRST-01 | 2026-11-14 | Owner, from his conversations: the first computer action of a member after the computer slept failed at least nine times (`docker_stopped`, `driver_configuration_failed`, `task_stopped`; a member told him 「電腦沒在運作」 while it ran) and 「再試一次」 made it work with nothing changed. The first call after a wake waits for the computer to be truly ready inside the one tool call, and looks again at the states that are only a computer on its way up. (1) `VmTools._awake` runs the member's start again, after a growing pause, at most `start_attempts` = 3 tries and `start_budget_s` = 150 seconds since the first, while it fails with `TRANSIENT_START` (`docker_stopped`, `unavailable`, `start_failed`, `health_timeout`); the repair of a lost connection does the same and may bring Docker Desktop back as the start may (it ran without that permission and answered `docker_stopped` for an engine that was only restarting). (2) `VmTools._configured` waits out `driver_configuration_failed` (a driver that has not finished starting refuses its first set-up calls), `connect_attempts` = 3 with a pause, before the repair. (3) A work item whose first call was cut short (its task cancelled while it waited for a sleeping computer) was marked stopped by the viewer's poll and every later call of that turn failed in milliseconds with `task_stopped`: a stop that was only read off the task (`Work.inferred`) is revived by the next live call of the same turn; a stop the owner said (a takeover stopped or expired) is not inferred and stays. (4) What is not transient is reported at once and never tried again: Docker not installed, Docker Desktop that could not be launched or did not come up in its two minutes, no image, an image that does not fit, no room, the owner holding control, the owner's Stop, a computer the member no longer has. | pytest | hard | core/tests/computer/test_first_call.py::test_a_computer_still_on_its_way_up_is_waited_for_inside_the_one_call; core/tests/computer/test_first_call.py::test_two_starts_in_a_row_that_are_not_ready_are_still_one_call; core/tests/computer/test_first_call.py::test_a_computer_that_never_comes_up_is_given_up_on_after_a_bounded_number_of_tries; core/tests/computer/test_first_call.py::test_the_wait_ends_at_its_time_bound_even_with_tries_left; core/tests/computer/test_first_call.py::test_a_real_failure_is_reported_at_once_and_never_tried_again; core/tests/computer/test_first_call.py::test_a_driver_that_is_not_configured_yet_is_waited_for; core/tests/computer/test_first_call.py::test_a_driver_that_is_never_configured_is_reported_after_a_bounded_number_of_tries; core/tests/computer/test_first_call.py::test_a_first_call_that_was_cut_short_does_not_stop_the_rest_of_its_turn; core/tests/computer/test_first_call.py::test_a_stop_the_owner_said_is_still_a_stop |
| R-COMP-OWN-01 | 2026-11-14 | Owner, 2026-11-13 and -14 (four turns): saying 「用你的電腦開…」 into the overlay meant Raphael's own computer, the member's seat, but the fast router took 「開」 as the fixed command that opens things and opened the page on the owner's own PC, and the reply was empty. (1) A quick request that names the member's computer (你的電腦, 你自己的電腦, 你用電腦, your computer, あなたのパソコン, Raphael's) never takes the shortcut: `AgentService._answer` skips the router and the turn is the member's ordinary turn, which does the work on its own computer; for that turn the owner's PC is out of reach (`_Ask.own_computer`, `permissions.owner_pc(own_computer=...)`, the local-tools door and the prompt), so nothing is widened and nothing falls back to it. A sentence that names both computers is the member's. 「我的電腦」, 這台電腦, my computer, 本機 are the owner's and keep the shortcut. (2) An open request that names no one's computer (「用電腦開 YouTube」, open it on the computer) is asked once, in one line and at no cost (`which_computer`: this PC, or {name}'s own); the reply names a side or agrees, and is never asked twice, an agreeing or repeating reply is the member's. (3) When the open shortcut runs it says what it did in one line, never an empty reply (`opened`: 「已在你的電腦打開「youtube.com」。」, an app by the name the Start menu shows). Only a quick question reaches the owner's PC, as R-PC-01 says. | pytest | hard | core/tests/agent/test_whose_computer.py::test_a_request_that_names_the_members_computer_is_the_members; core/tests/agent/test_whose_computer.py::test_a_request_that_names_the_owners_pc_is_the_owners; core/tests/agent/test_whose_computer.py::test_a_request_that_names_no_ones_computer_is_unsure; core/tests/agent/test_whose_computer.py::test_a_request_about_no_computer_is_left_to_the_router; core/tests/agent/test_whose_computer.py::test_the_reply_to_which_computer_names_a_side; core/tests/agent/test_whose_computer.py::test_a_quick_request_for_the_members_computer_goes_to_its_turn_not_to_the_owners_pc; core/tests/agent/test_whose_computer.py::test_the_member_that_has_no_computer_says_so_and_still_does_not_borrow_the_owners; core/tests/agent/test_whose_computer.py::test_the_owners_own_pc_still_opens_the_page_when_the_owner_says_so_and_says_so_back; core/tests/agent/test_whose_computer.py::test_the_shortcut_says_in_one_line_what_it_did; core/tests/agent/test_whose_computer.py::test_an_app_that_was_opened_is_named_as_the_start_menu_names_it; core/tests/agent/test_whose_computer.py::test_a_request_that_names_no_ones_computer_is_asked_once_and_costs_nothing; core/tests/agent/test_whose_computer.py::test_the_members_own_computer_answers_without_asking_again |

## Reliability found in the owner's real use (2026-11-14)

Six findings of the owner's database from 2026-11-01 to 2026-11-14 (a copy, read-only; no text of it is kept here).

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-CLI-SIGNIN-03 | 2026-11-14 | Eight turns on the Claude CLI failed `auth_expired` in three days, three of them 07:00 routines started together, and the owner had no warning. A subscription CLI that refuses its sign-in (`expired` or `signed_out`) is remembered (`CliBrains.sign_in_lost`) until a run of it is answered, a sign-in finishes in the app's console, its credential files change (a sign-in done anywhere; only their modification time is read) or thirty minutes pass. A routine run that would start meanwhile on that CLI is not started into the same refusal: it ends at once, unstarted, with the cause the first run told (reason `cli_login_skipped`, the same sentence 「Claude Code 的登入已過期。」 and the same turn failure `cause`), so the button of R-CLI-SIGNIN-02 stands under it and mends them all with one sign-in; after it a finished sign-in runs a failed task (a routine's run) again by the core in place of asking the routine's title as a question. Routine runs on one CLI start at least `ROUTINE_CLI_GAP_S` (10 s) apart, so the first run's refusal is known before the next starts. A task the owner handed over is never refused by this, and an answered one clears it. The allowance warning below 10% already exists for Claude (R-QUOTA-01: its `rate_limit_event` feeds `Quotas.record`); nothing new is asked of the CLI. | pytest, vitest | hard | core/tests/agent/test_cli_signin_cause.py::test_a_refused_sign_in_is_remembered_until_something_says_it_is_back; core/tests/agent/test_cli_signin_cause.py::test_a_sign_in_that_finished_in_the_apps_own_console_clears_the_refusal; core/tests/background/test_routine_places.py::test_routines_after_a_refused_sign_in_are_not_run_into_it_and_say_the_cause_once; core/tests/background/test_routine_places.py::test_the_next_routine_asks_the_cli_again_once_a_sign_in_is_back; core/tests/background/test_routine_places.py::test_owner_chat_is_never_refused_by_the_routine_gate; app/src/main/cli-signin.test.ts::a finished sign-in runs a failed task again by the core (a routine's run), and asks the question again only for a reply with no task |
| R-ROUTINE-11 | 2026-11-14 | Five routines due at 07:00 started together and a check timed out on a PC that was still starting. Routine runs take at most `ROUTINE_PARALLEL` (2) of the `MAX_PARALLEL` (3) places; the rest queue in the order they came and start as places free. A place always stays for what the owner handed over, which is also taken before a waiting routine, so the owner's task never queues behind routines (it still waits for its own member's running work, one task a member as before). | R-SKILL-12 | 2026-11-14 | Five turns of 2026-11-06 to 2026-11-09 (712, 727, 739, 856, 1254) were answered in 5 seconds to 1.3 minutes by their own models, yet each showed a last message 5 to 35 hours later. A skill learned for a turn that is no longer running (`MembersService.skill_learned`) is still drawn in that turn, but its notice is `activity_only`: it redraws the thread and is never news (no notice, no light, no unread mark); a turn still under way, whose own work learned it, arrives as before. | pytest | hard | core/tests/members/test_skill_growth.py::test_a_skill_learned_hours_after_its_turn_is_a_line_in_it_and_not_that_turn_arriving_again |

| R-TOOL-02 | 2026-11-14 | The same routine then ran 143 steps until the connector's query allowance was spent and changed no card. Owner direction (2026-11-14): members persist like Hermes Agent, so no run is ended for failing: it tries other ways until its own budget ends it. A call of a connected app that has failed twice in a row with the same error (same tool, same arguments) is not made a third time (`TaskRun._repeat_refusal`; only an error that is the same every time counts, `tool_error` and `bad_arguments`, never a wait, a timeout, a missing yes or a lost connection). The model is told, as the call's result (kind `repeat_failed`, shown whole), that the call was not made, what the error said (300 characters), the top-level arguments the tool takes, and to try a different way: other arguments, another tool or another source. A call with other arguments, another tool, or one that went well is made as before; the refused repeat counts in the task's step budget like any call, which alone ends a run (the limits of `RunLimits` are unchanged), and it is no refusal that fails a routine (R-ROUTINE-05). | pytest | hard | core/tests/background/test_argument_shape.py::test_the_same_call_that_failed_twice_with_the_same_error_is_not_made_again_and_the_run_goes_on; core/tests/background/test_argument_shape.py::test_a_call_that_fails_in_the_shape_it_takes_is_told_the_shape_again_when_it_is_repeated; core/tests/background/test_argument_shape.py::test_a_background_task_goes_on_after_a_repeated_failure_and_ends_only_by_its_own_report |
| R-ROUTINE-10 | 2026-11-14 | A member's mail routine, every three hours, found no new mail 39 times and spent about nine tool steps each time. A routine with a schedule may carry a precheck (`precheck` of the member's `routine_create`; changed or removed (`{}`) through `routines.update` and the owner's `member_routine_update`, not in the member's own `routine_update`, whose words the default prompt's budget cannot spare): `{app, tool: mcp:server/tool, arguments}`, a read tool of a connected app, named by whoever sets the routine up; nothing in the core knows any app. When the routine is due the scheduler calls it with no model (the event routines' gate: a tool that writes, one above the read tiers, one the member may not use or an app that is not connected is refused when the routine is set, and at run time reads nothing) and compares a hash of the result with the one kept from the run before: the same result skips the run quietly (logged as skipped, `last_skipped`; no turn, no message, no notice, no run row and no count toward `max_runs` or the hourly limits), a different one wakes the model and is kept once the run was made. The first run, a precheck that cannot be read, and a manual test run are always made; changing or removing the precheck forgets the hash; an event routine has none. The field and the calendar's limit cost 45 tokens of the default prompt, inside its measured budgets (API 16048, CLI 15212). | pytest | hard | core/tests/members/test_routine_precheck.py::test_a_precheck_is_kept_shown_and_changed_by_the_routine_tools; core/tests/members/test_routine_precheck.py::test_only_the_form_of_a_read_tool_call_is_a_precheck; core/tests/members/test_routine_precheck.py::test_a_tool_that_writes_or_is_not_connected_is_no_precheck_and_an_event_routine_has_none; core/tests/members/test_routine_precheck.py::test_the_first_run_is_made_an_unchanged_result_skips_the_next_quietly_and_a_change_wakes_it_again; core/tests/members/test_routine_precheck.py::test_a_precheck_that_cannot_be_read_never_holds_the_run_back; core/tests/members/test_routine_precheck.py::test_a_precheck_taken_away_or_changed_starts_from_nothing_and_a_manual_test_run_ignores_it; core/tests/members/test_routine_precheck.py::test_skipped_runs_do_not_use_up_a_routines_run_count; core/tests/members/test_management_discovery.py::test_default_production_prompt_stays_below_the_measured_pre_unit_budget |

## Only credentials and payment secrets are secret (owner, 2026-11-14)

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-SECRET-01 | 2026-11-14 | Owner (2026-11-14, after his member 紫苑, a Japanese teacher, could not show an N1 study page three times and gave up: 「App 頁面系統把內容中的文字誤判為機密資訊」): 「我覺得現在的機密資訊鎖得太死了，理論上根本不用鎖這麼死。我們這是開源的東西，不用這麼嚴謹。你全部都打開了，不用引用太多機密資訊，除非是什麼密碼啊、金鑰啊，不然是什麼 credit cards 的密碼什麼的，那個才是機密啊」. Only real credentials and payment secrets are secret, and nothing else is blocked or masked as one: URLs, an ID or a number inside a URL, article text, hashes, file names, personal facts, a national ID number. `memory/scan.py` `find_secret` (and `redact`, which masks the same things in notes, skills, tool transcripts and a template's findings) finds: a key or token of a known shape (`sk-or-`/`sk-proj-`/`sk-ant-`, a bare `sk-` followed by one run of 20 or more letters and digits, `ghp_`, `github_pat_`, `xox`, `AKIA`, `AIza`, a JWT, `Bearer`, a PEM private key, a Telegram bot token); a value of eight characters or more, with a digit or the case changes of random letters, given to a word for a key (`api_key`, `token`, `secret`, `client_secret`, `access_token`, 金鑰, 密鑰, APIキー, …) by `:`, `=`, 是, 為, は or "is"; what a word for a password, a PIN, a one-time code or a CVV gives its value to (any value after `:`, `=`, "is" or "was"; after 是, 為 or は only a quoted value or one that begins in ASCII, so that パスワードは大切です, 暗証番号4桁を入力してください and 密碼是用來登入的 are sentences, not passwords); a card number (13 to 19 digits, first digit 2 to 6, Luhn, never the digits of a decimal, a word or an address's path or query); a bank account number (an IBAN, in one run or in groups of four, whose ISO 7064 check digits hold, or the 8 to 20 digits a word for a bank account gives its number to). A string that merely looks random is never a secret: the generic long-random-run check, the readable-path exception it needed (R-PAGE-05) and the Taiwan national ID number check are gone. A key of a known shape or a labelled credential inside a link (`?key=AIza…`, `?token=…`) is still one. The guard against steering a model (`find_injection`, applied to a memory at its write and when a turn reads it; never to a page, a note's text or a report) no longer refuses a link with a query string, or a picture at an address with none: the owner's links are shown, not obeyed. It still refuses an order to ignore the instructions, a line dressed as a system message, an order to send something to an address, a Markdown image whose address carries a query (a way to send data out) and invisible characters. Callers checked: memory writes and the prompt-time check (`memory/store.py`), the old-store merge (`memory/legacy.py`), `show_page`/`update_page` title, html, fallback text and tool data (`agent/pages.py checked_text`), report text, summary and link addresses (`agent/report_page.py`), notes, skills and the member's skill files (`bots/homes.py redact_secrets`), private tool transcripts (`memory/tool_events.py`), screen teaching (`computer/teaching.py`), a template's findings (`members/templates.py`) and the owner-private team report (`members/team_work.py`). Unchanged on purpose: the gate on a member exported as a recipe to be passed on (`members/template_author.py PRIVATE`: an email address, a path on the owner's disk, an address on his own network, a labelled account or owner name), the names a skill's folder may not hold (`members/capabilities.py _SECRET_FILE`), the vault, `request_secret` and the floor that keeps commands from the app's database and vault. | pytest | hard | core/tests/memory/test_scan.py::test_the_shapes_of_keys_and_tokens_are_secrets; ## Reordering the sidebar follows the pointer (owner, 2026-11-14)

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-SIDE-03 | 2026-11-14 | Owner: 「左邊的 sidebar，為什麼它只是調順序而已也會卡？你的設計可能有一些問題，理論上這邊都是 UI 的調整，不應該會卡才對」. Measured, a drop sent one members.update per member of the folder (or one groups.update per folder), each waited for the core's answer (about 16 ms of its own work on a fresh database with fifteen members, `sync_homes` writing every member's home), each announced a change that read the whole roster again and drew the panel, the settings page and the conversation again: the row stood where it was dropped about 370 ms later in the mock core, three reads and five saves for one move. Now a row or a folder stands where it is dropped in the same task (`orderMs` under 150 ms, measured 10 to 60 ms), saved with one request (`members.update {group_id?, position}` or `groups.update {position}`; `position` is a place among every member but Raphael, `movedMember` in member-order.ts numbering as store.py `_move` does, pinned in core/tests/members/test_service.py). Only the moved row is a new node (and only when it changes folder: its face is kept); the rows and faces beside it, the folders and the conversation are the same nodes before and after. Moves wait for each other, and a read begun before a move or while it is saved is not drawn over it (R-SLIME-02). The core's answer and its change event that only confirm the order draw nothing but the column's patch (`withoutPlaces`: places, folders, the roster's order and revision are left out of the comparison, so no panel, settings page, conversation or Library read follows). A save that fails puts the column back, says why in the toast, and reads the core again. A move to where the row stands asks nothing; the hidden folder takes no drop. | vitest, pytest, look | hard | app/src/main/member-order.test.ts; app/src/main/pages/agent-members.test.ts::a row or a folder dropped on the sidebar; core/tests/members/test_service.py::test_one_update_puts_a_member_in_a_folder_at_a_place_among_all_members_and_numbers_them_again; sidebar-reorder-members; sidebar-reorder-across; sidebar-reorder-folder; app/scripts/look/drag-probe.mjs; app/scripts/look/drag-probe.test.mjs; app/scripts/look/mock-ipc.test.mjs |

## The sidebar's lights (owner, 2026-11-14)

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-SIDE-04 | 2026-11-14 | Owner: 「左邊提示燈的部分，他們如果在思考的情況下，不要有提示他們有新的動作，因為我這樣點過去，他們其實是沒有任何內容。只有他們真正傳出訊息的時候，我左邊才會有提示。那個提示燈可能需要設計一下。其實現在我蠻難去追蹤每一個成員的進度，所以你可能要確定一下，有可能會有不同顏色的，像是白色 maybe 是它傳了一個新的 message，然後綠色可能是他完成任務之類的。可能要有一套邏輯」. Cause: the core marked a thread unread at every `agent.turn` event of a turn that is written, and a running turn is written when it begins and again at each step (a routine's too, the moment it starts), so a member that only thought had a dot and nothing to read behind it. Now a turn that is still running marks its thread only once it has sent a message (a `reply`; a progress line is none), and the roster says what the unseen news is (`members.list` `unread_kind`, `rooms.list` `unread_kind`: `message`; `done` for a turn of a task or a routine that ended well, and for a group chat whose meeting ended with words the owner has not read; `failed` for a turn or task that ended in error, expiry, a limit or an interruption, the owner's own no or stop excepted; `failed` outranks `done` outranks `message`). A sidebar row (a member, a group chat, a pinned face, on the rail the face's corner) wears one still dot, the strongest of: orange (`--wait`, a question or an approval waits for the owner: `waiting` or the activity `blocked`; it stays until it is answered), red (`--err`, failed, or a run that failed unseen), green (`--ok`, done), white (`--text`, the theme's own foreground, a new message), none (nothing new: a member that thinks or works says nothing, its slime shows the work). The dot keeps the size and place it had (`--pa-dot`), never animates, has no tooltip (its words would repeat the row) and names itself for a screen reader: 等你處理, 需要你看一下, 已完成, 有新訊息. Opening the conversation reads white, green and red at once (`lightOf(row, open)`; the core clears them with `members.read`), and orange stays while its question waits. The four colours keep 3:1 on every surface a row stands on, selected, hovered and pressed too (check-palette.mjs `sidebarLights`). The header's member picker shows one dot for all the other members, the strongest of their lights. | vitest, pytest, look | hard | app/src/main/sidebar-light.test.ts::a member that is thinking or working has none, nothing new to read; app/src/main/sidebar-light.test.ts::opening the conversation reads white, green and red, and an orange question stays while it waits; core/tests/members/test_service.py::test_a_member_that_is_thinking_is_not_news_until_it_has_sent_something; core/tests/members/test_service.py::test_a_routine_that_starts_is_not_news_and_one_that_ends_is_done; core/tests/members/test_service.py::test_a_failed_turn_is_failed_news_and_a_reply_after_it_does_not_hide_it; core/tests/members/test_service.py::test_unread_kind_ranks_failed_over_done_over_message; core/tests/members/test_rooms.py::test_a_room_says_what_its_news_is_and_a_finished_meeting_is_done; sidebar-lights; sidebar-lights-rail; sidebar-lights-pinned; sidebar-lights-thinking; sidebar-lights-open; sidebar-unread; app/scripts/check-palette.mjs |

## The owner can always take the computer, and a hold never outlives its use (2026-11-14)

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-COMP-TAKE-01 | 2026-11-14 | Owner, with a picture of the larger view of a seat showing a page in Chrome, its pill reading only 觀看中: 「為什麼我沒辦法接手啊 ? 理論上不管甚麼時候我都應該要可以接手 ?」. The owner can take the computer whenever he asks: from watching, while a member works, after an earlier take-over, after reopening the view. Cause in the code (the exact path of his picture is not proven, the mechanisms are reproduced): the larger view hid 接手控制 whenever the core said the lease was the owner's (a condition of not watching, not running, owner or demonstration) while the pill said 觀看中 whoever held it, so a lease the view was not using (a take-over the screen never confirmed whose give-back failed, a view of his own that left it behind) left a pill with no way out; and the core refused a second view's Take control with "desktop already held" for as long as the first view's hold stood, a hold nobody kept included, and with "desktop busy" whenever a member's action happened to be under way when the press landed, after which the view, having left watching, hid 接手控制 for good (the same dead end, reached by one press while a member works). The look check `desktop-watch` had skipped the button's assertion in exactly that state. Now a pill that reads 觀看中 always offers 接手控制 (the computer runs, nothing is being recorded), and one press asks the core for the lease; the core gives it: another view of his own that holds, or a hold that no view keeps and no sign-in request waits on, is his to take, the replaced view is revoked at once and its keys released. Only a recording in the old holder's view refuses. A press that lands while a member's action is under way holds the screen at once (the member's next action is refused as "the owner has the screen"), gives that action up to `TAKE_WAIT_S` = 10 seconds to end, then takes; only an action that outlasts that is answered "desktop busy", the hold is given back as it was, the pill reads 正在接手… while the core decides and 觀看中 with 接手控制 again after a refusal. The owner's hold turns members away only while it is real, and the member is told what is real. (1) A hold never outlives its holder: closing a view released the computer's hold as its last step, after awaits that can fail (the relay, the recorder, the sockets), and the monitor that closes every expired view was one loop that a single failing view ended for good, so a hold could stand with no view behind it until the core restarted, turning every member away and every later Take control down; the release is now in a `finally` and the monitor carries on past a view it cannot close. (2) How long a lease lives: while his view is open and renewed, until he hands it back or his view closes (a closed view releases at once; an app that vanished, within 15 seconds of its last renewal); and since 2026-11-14 an owner's hold with no click or key of his (counted from the relay's forwarded input) for `OWNER_IDLE_S` = 300 seconds is handed back by itself, the view staying open and watching; a sign-in or payment request that waits for him and a recording are his until he says so. (3) A member turned away by the owner's hold is told in one sentence within the 240 characters a failure keeps, in every path (API, worker, CLI bridge): who holds the screen, for how long, that nothing was done, when it ends, and not to guess another cause; a waiting sign-in request says that instead. | pytest | hard | core/tests/computer/test_viewer.py::test_a_view_that_fails_to_close_still_gives_the_hold_back; core/tests/computer/test_viewer.py::test_one_view_that_cannot_close_does_not_end_the_watch_over_the_others; core/tests/computer/test_viewer.py::test_a_lease_the_owner_stopped_using_is_released_but_a_touched_or_waited_for_one_is_not; core/tests/computer/test_vm.py::test_tools_share_snapshot_and_gate_and_owner_control |

## A computer wakes by a decision, waits for a loaded PC, keeps every panel whole (owner, 2026-11-14)

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-COMP-WAKE-01 | 2026-11-14 | Owner: 「電腦的部分你可能要再改好一下…你就一次把所有東西都修復吧」. A sleeping computer wakes in two ways only: a member's own computer tool call decides a task needs it (a turn, a routine or a background task alike), or the owner starts it. Opening a conversation or a panel never does, and reading a page never does: `web_read` falls back to the member's browser only when the computer is already awake; asleep, it returns the page as read with `READ_NEEDS_BROWSER` (or the failure with that instruction), so the member chooses `browser_open` itself. A start by a member's tool is never silent: one quiet line in that member's conversation, an event and never a message of the member's (R-TEXT-08; `vm_tools.py::WOKE`, a progress line where no conversation takes notices) names the member and why (a page, a command, or using the computer) in every interface language, with no toast; the member's panel says nothing of it, since the computer is the third tab only (R-PANEL-06; the core still reports `woken` in the viewer's status, cleared when it sleeps or the owner starts it, and the app no longer reads it). The owner watching the stream is activity: a viewer with a connected socket, a preview included, keeps the computer from the idle bound; unwatched and quiet it still sleeps at the bound. | pytest, vitest, look | hard | core/tests/computer/test_wake.py::test_a_members_tool_that_wakes_the_computer_says_who_and_why_once; core/tests/computer/test_wake.py::test_the_owners_own_start_is_silent_and_clears_the_last_wake; core/tests/computer/test_wake.py::test_reading_a_page_never_wakes_a_sleeping_computer; core/tests/computer/test_wake.py::test_reading_a_page_uses_a_computer_that_is_already_awake; core/tests/computer/test_wake.py::test_the_wake_line_is_written_in_every_interface_language; core/tests/computer/test_viewer.py::test_a_computer_the_owner_is_watching_is_not_idle_and_one_nobody_watches_sleeps; core/tests/agent/test_web_read_hint.py::test_a_sleeping_computer_is_not_woken_to_read_a_page_and_the_member_is_told_to_choose; app/src/main/desktop-viewer.test.ts; computer-wake-line |
| R-COMP-LOAD-01 | 2026-11-14 | Owner: 「電腦的部分你可能要再改好一下…你就一次把所有東西都修復吧」. A browser's first window is waited for by the clock, not by a count of looks: raphael-seat-browser gives it 60 s and 30 s to settle and says how long; every bound outside it is longer (seat_browser_s 120, browser_open_s 300 over the carry's 75 s), browser_prepare has the read deadline, and a moved window is waited for 10 s. ## What the core sends the computer's driver (2026-11-12)

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |

| R-COMP-DRIVER-01 | 2026-11-12 | Found while proving the shared computer in real containers: from 2026-11-04 the connection set-up sent `set_agent_cursor_enabled` a `cursor_id` the driver's schema does not name, the driver refused it, and every screen and browser tool of every member's computer answered `driver_configuration_failed`. The fake drivers of the tests took any argument. Their measured fixture now retains every validation keyword, removing only descriptive annotations. Browser fakes also return the measured authorization_host_failed refusal for target-only get_browser_state and refuse browser_navigate outside http/https/about; schema validation alone does not express these runtime constraints. A set-up call carries only what `cua-driver describe` names (`enabled` and `session` for the cursor, `max_image_dimension` and `session` for the config). A driver session label is never used twice: the driver of a computer that kept running refuses a label that has ended, so a core started again does not count from 1 once more. `npm run verify -- --computer` makes the product's own connection twice on a real computer (the second as a core started again) and calls a tool through it. | pytest | hard | core/tests/computer/test_latency.py::test_window_reads_are_bounded_and_driver_images_and_cursor_are_configured; core/tests/computer/test_latency.py::test_a_core_started_again_never_reuses_a_driver_session_label; core/tests/evaluate/test_computer_acceptance.py::test_driver_check_makes_the_products_own_connection_with_arguments_the_driver_accepts; core/tests/evaluate/test_computer_acceptance.py::test_driver_check_fails_when_the_driver_refuses_a_set_up_argument; core/tests/evaluate/test_computer_acceptance.py::test_driver_check_starts_no_driver_process_in_another_owners_container |
| R-COMP-DRIVER-02 | 2026-11-13 | Found with real members on the shared computer (2026-11-12): a member's first use of its computer started its CLI turn again with "The computer tool catalog changed", the first call's result was lost, and the member went on guessing window and tab ids. The list kept with the code (`driver_schemas.json`) was older than the driver in the image: all 30 tools had other parameters, some the driver refuses, so asking the driver replaced every one. The kept list is the pinned driver's own `tools/list` for the offered tools, and it and a running driver's list are read by one function (`vm_catalog.definition`): asking the pinned driver changes nothing a member was offered, to the byte, and what a call is checked against before the driver is asked is the driver's own schema. A driver that lists something else is still taken, the one time the list changes. Found beside it: a description is cut at 400 characters and every computer tool opened with the same approval rule, more than twice that long, so all thirty-three read as its same opening words and none said what it did (a member sent PowerShell to its Linux shell). A computer tool's description is shown whole (`PluginTool.whole`), opens with what the tool is for and carries the approval rule after it; the working guidance that was cut off unseen is in the member's instructions, once. The driver's own description is not offered (its parameters' words are). The shared computer's sentence is on the four tools it is about. Every request of every member carries these tools, so their descriptions' total length is bounded by a test. `npm run verify -- --computer` compares the running driver's list with the kept one (words and order included), asks the driver through `VmTools` and requires the offer unchanged, and writes the list beside its report (`driver-tools.json`), from which `generate_driver_tools.py` writes both kept files. | pytest | hard | core/tests/computer/test_catalog_stays.py; core/tests/evaluate/test_computer_acceptance.py::test_live_schema_check_requires_the_kept_list_to_be_the_drivers_own; core/tests/evaluate/test_computer_acceptance.py::test_live_schema_check_fails_when_asking_the_driver_changes_the_offer |
| R-COMP-READY-01 | 2026-11-13 | Owner, of Grok Bot's computer: 「他們會有 chrome 等等之類的 我們一開始就要把環境架設好了 不會只有 cua」. Nothing in a computer is root, so a member cannot add a system package: the image is where its software comes from. Image layer 8 adds, to the base's curl, ffmpeg, node and Python: git, wget, zip, unzip, jq, ripgrep, npm, sqlite3, poppler-utils, build-essential, less, nano, tree, file, and Debian's Noto CJK and colour emoji fonts (until then only the Japanese cut was there and Chinese was drawn in its shapes). The members' browser is Google's own Chrome where Google builds one (amd64), taken from Google's repository under Google's key at the version current when the image is built; on arm64 it stays Chromium. The core knows the browser's windows by either name (`vm_catalog.BROWSER_APPS`). Every computer is made again at its next start, as with each layer; workspaces, sign-ins and files stay. A layer is made again only when `vm.known_previous` names it in the shape it was released in: layer 7 (a computer of a member's own, and the six-screen one every member shares) was added with layer 8 after the acceptance run found such a computer refused as incompatible, and each new layer adds the one before it. The acceptance run makes a computer of every released layer whose image is on the PC, the shared one of layer 7 among them, and starts it on the new layer. | pytest, computer acceptance | hard | core/tests/computer/test_seat_tools.py::test_the_shared_computer_of_layer_seven_is_made_again_on_the_next_layer; core/tests/computer/test_seat_tools.py::test_the_computer_starts_with_googles_chrome_the_tools_and_the_fonts; core/tests/computer/test_seat_tools.py::test_launcher_difference_excludes_disappeared_windows_and_attributes_same_pid_popups; core/tests/evaluate/test_computer_acceptance.py::test_shared_acceptance_drives_real_tools_through_every_numbered_check |

Image layer 9: every screen's launcher and the menu's one browser entry carry the installed browser's own name and mark (Google Chrome on amd64, Chromium on arm64), named in the image build after the browser is installed, and the build stops when the mark is not among the icons; XFCE's helper, the menu's Chrome and Chromium entries and `x-www-browser` all start `raphael-browser`; Firefox was left installed and out of the menu (R-COMP-READY-03 removes it on layer 10). The step is after the browser's own in the Dockerfile, so a PC that built layer 8 builds layer 9 from what it kept. Layer 8, a member's own computer and the six-screen one every member shares, is named in `vm.known_previous` and made again on layer 9 at its next start. `npm run verify -- --computer` reads every screen's launcher, the helper, the menu's entries and `x-www-browser` and opens XFCE's "Web Browser" to see which window comes ("N screens browser mark"); the shared block reads its six launchers; a layer 8 image on the PC is started on layer 9 in both shapes.
