| R-PAGE-08 | 2026-11-14 | Owner request (2026-11-14, on Grok Bot's Library, where a page's row opens the page, not the thread at its reply: 「我不是說這個右邊點下去，不是要快速連接到原本對話的位置嗎？它是要可以直接打開檔案，就是跟我們平常看到的不太一樣。它是會直接打開那個檔案本身，我不知道你懂不懂，然後它會一樣顯示在 app 裡面，然後它有一個share的鍵，你可以看一下 grokbot 是怎麼做的」). A page, an HTML page or a report, opens as the page itself and is the whole area right of the sidebar. **Opening.** A press on a page in the member's Library (R-PANEL-02) opens it whole at once, as a press on its card in the thread does (R-PAGE-02, R-PAGE-03: one view, one way back): the details panel folds away, the view stands with the page's title in the header, and the page takes its place when its card has read it. The press remembers what it found and the way back puts it all back: the details panel on the tab it was on, the conversation that was open and the thread where it was read (the window of turns and the scroll). A page of an older conversation of the member opens that conversation quietly under the page (its turn is read around the page's, no turn marked) and the arrow brings back the conversation that was open; if the owner wrote a message about the page, that conversation, where the exchange is, stays. The card stays the page's one home (its frame, its revisions, what the member is told it is called: `pages.viewed_by` is unchanged, the same conversation), so the thread stands hidden behind the view; a page whose card does not come in ten seconds or cannot be read leaves nothing open and puts everything back, with a word. **The area.** The details panel is folded whenever a page stands (by the Library's press or the card's), and comes back with the page's way out. An HTML page is the whole width of the view, edge to edge (R-PAGE-10; until 2026-11-14 it stood up to `--pgv-w`, 1280 px, between the thread's gutters), with no frame, corner, edge or box of its own and no line of title: the whole area under the header scrolls as one and the page does not, its frame as tall as the page says (up to 20000 px, the page's own height messages; a card keeps its 2400) and at least as tall as the area, so a page made to fill a screen fills it. It fades out under the header as the thread does, and the composer stays below it, never over it, as wide as the thread's column. A report keeps its column (its own drawing and contents), also scrolling as the whole area, its background the whole view's (R-PAGE-10). **The header.** While a page stands the centre is the page's own pill (its mark and its title, one soft pill, no control) in place of the member's pill, which comes back with the thread (a room's heading keeps its name and faces); the round arrow named 返回 stays at the left as the way out (R-UI-117) and Share is at the right (R-PAGE-01). **Share.** One Share button, first of the header's tools, on every page: a report is saved as its file as before, an HTML page as the file the core builds of the page it keeps (the window names no folder and gives no text), and a plugin's page, which works through its host, has none. **Voice.** The flyer stands over an open page (R-VC-04) as before. | pytest, vitest, look | hard | core/tests/agent/test_pages.py::test_an_html_page_file_is_the_page_without_the_window_helper_under_the_pages_own_policy; ## Subscription quota warning (2026-11-10)

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |

| R-QUOTA-01 | 2026-11-10 | Owner decision 5 of the 2026-11-09 cache and accounting proposal: when a subscription window (Codex, Claude, Antigravity; 5 hours or weekly) has 10% or less left, the owner is told once per provider, period and reset time, whoever read it (a CLI turn, a background task or the usage page). The main window shows one toast with the subscription, the window, what is left and when it resets; a hidden window shows it when it is next seen, and not at all once that window has reset. A spent window (its own notice says so), a report of a window already over, a subscription blocked by another window and a reading no one hears tell nothing. The `quota_warned` setting keeps what was told, so a restart says nothing again and the next window after a reset is told again; a told window read again with 20% or more left (a voucher, a reset that kept its end) is told again when it falls to the last tenth. ## Where a slime moves, and a screen that never flickers (2026-11-11)

Owner request of 2026-11-11 (「現在不知道為什麼，它有時候更新字幕啊，或者是更新什麼東西，它就會閃現，就會亂跳一通，可能文字會突然看不到…我希望整體的畫面永遠保持正常顯示，不要有任何問題。然後在工作的時候，只要有左邊的，就是可以看到所有成員的那個地方，讓它有動畫就可以了。還有那個它在思考的，就是在對話框思考那個，也要有動畫。那其他上面顯示的，跟右邊 sidebar 的，這邊都不用有動畫，不然我覺得會有點奇怪，要有所取捨。有些地方就是放照片，就是史萊姆的樣子就可以了」). This section supersedes every place in R-UI-74, R-UI-99 and R-UI-78 that lets a slime move in the header, the panel, a card, a group, a dialog or the guide.

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-SLIME-01 | 2026-11-11 | A slime moves in exactly two places: a member's row (or pinned tile) in the left member list, while that member works, and the thinking or waiting line of the open conversation (a private thread or a group). Everywhere else it is a picture of that slime: the conversation header, the details panel, the member settings page, group faces and their header cluster, hand-off and other cards, the new-member maker and the slime picker, mentions, dialogs, the guide's slimes, the computer's boot face and the search. A picture keeps its colour, hat, crown and accessories and the pose of rest (idle, or sleeping after a day's silence); it has no CSS animation, no transition, no strips, no clock and no state to hand over, and nothing observes it. `faceHTML` and `slimeFaceHTML` draw a picture unless they are given `moving`, and only the member row, the pinned tile and the thinking line give it. At a compact width the list is a drawer: while it is closed no slime is drawn there, so the look check asks for a moving one in the list only while the list is on screen. | vitest, look | hard | app/src/main/slime.test.ts::a slime is a picture unless it moves: whatever its member does, it keeps its resting pose and no clock; app/src/main/slime.test.ts::a picture keeps the states that are poses without motion, asleep and at rest, and a wait for the owner is only a slime at rest; app/src/main/slime.test.ts::faceHTML moves only when asked, for Raphael and for a member alike; app/src/main/slime.test.ts::the pictures of the picker, the maker and the stores never move; app/src/main/slime.test.ts::slime.css holds a picture still against every rule that could move it; app/src/main/ui-patch.test.ts::a picture of a slime is kept as it is by a redraw, and another member takes a fresh one (owner, 2026-11-11); app/src/main/slime-offscreen.test.ts::a picture of a slime is never watched, so it costs the page nothing (owner, 2026-11-11); app/scripts/look/slime-places.test.mjs::a slime in the member list or the thinking line may move, and one anywhere else must be a picture; app/scripts/look/slime-places.test.mjs::a list that is not on screen is not asked to move, and one that is on screen is; slime-places; slime-places-settings |
| R-SLIME-02 | 2026-11-11 | An update from the core touches only what changed. A member's step from one activity to another, or new words for what it is doing, change neither the identity of a thread line nor its face: a moving face is left out of a line's identity and the face already on screen is handed over by `setSlimeState` and `followSlimeRoster`, so the turn is not replaced, its words are not drawn again and nothing restarts. The conversations in the panel, the chip of the model and the model list are patched or left alone, never written anew, and the panel and the settings page do not draw again for a member's last line, time, unread mark or activity, nor does such a roster start a read of the panel's Library; a member's skills and commands are read when the slash menu is wanted, not at every roster event. The strips of a moving face (some thousands of nodes for a thinking or searching one) go in a slice of about 200 nodes a frame, out of sight, and show together, so that a change of state never holds a frame for 50 ms. A read that fails keeps what is on screen. | vitest, look | hard | app/src/main/thread-diff.test.ts::a line keeps its identity when only the state or the words of a member's face change; app/src/main/slime.test.ts::a roster read hands a face on screen over before the redraw, so the redraw keeps it; app/src/main/conversation-list.test.ts::a refresh patches the rows already on screen and a failed read keeps them; app/src/main/members.test.ts::a page about a member does not compare what changes with each word it says; app/src/main/pages/agent-members.test.ts::a roster that moves only a last line, a step or an unread mark starts no Library read and leaves the panel alone, and a renamed member reads and shows; app/src/main/slash-menu.test.ts::a member change reads nothing for a closed menu, and the next slash reads the skills it may have added; app/src/main/slime.test.ts::a heavy state's strips go in a slice a frame, out of sight, and show together, and a newer state drops the rest; app/scripts/look/event-storm.test.mjs::a storm that replaces a node, empties text, moves a scroll or animates a picture is told apart from a calm one; busy-team-calm |
| R-SLIME-03 | 2026-11-14 | Owner, with a picture of a member's reply that queued research in the background, an empty slime under it and, under that, a task line with the slime and its progress words (2026-11-14): 「這邊為什麼會有兩個史萊姆？理論上它不管怎麼樣都只會有一個史萊姆在運作才對，你可能確定一下」. One member shows at most one working slime in one conversation, whatever the number of turns, tasks, cards and messages on their way. `standingPresence` (timeline.ts) is the one rule: of the turns that want a slime (`presenceWant`: `words` while a turn or its task works or its replies are being read in, `bare` while it waits for the owner at a card, a question, a yes or a waiting task, nothing once it is over) the newest one that works stands, so a background task's line with its progress words wins over an empty slime; a face that only waits stands only when nothing works, and then the newest of those. Every other turn draws none (`TurnLook.presenceTurnId`). What the owner has just sent or is saying (a pending bubble, a voice message) is newer than every turn and takes the slime itself: the line under the newest of them, carrying the working turn's words, and no turn draws its own beside it. A line's cached drawing knows whether it holds the slime, so a line that gives it up is drawn again. A group's thinking lines are per member and per message, not a thread (R-AG-02). | vitest, look | hard | app/src/main/standing-presence.test.ts::a turn wants words while it works, a bare face while it waits, and nothing once it is over; app/src/main/standing-presence.test.ts::two tasks: the newest stands, the older line draws none; app/src/main/standing-presence.test.ts::a task with words stands before a newer face that only waits; app/src/main/pages/agent-stop.test.ts::a reply that handed work to the background shows one slime, on the task line; app/src/main/pages/agent-stop.test.ts::a turn that is running without a task shows one slime; app/src/main/pages/agent-stop.test.ts::two background tasks of one member show one slime, on the newest; app/src/main/pages/agent-stop.test.ts::a task line with words wins over a face that only waits, as in the owner's picture; app/src/main/pages/agent-stop.test.ts::a message just sent takes the one slime from a task that waits; slime-one-working |

## A model's link to a file it delivered (2026-11-14)

Owner, with a picture of a routine's message (member 專案經理, routine 「Notion 專案進度同步與排期（07:00 完整盤點）」) that ended with the words `[已查核的部分紀錄](attachment:4e43d6d3fd9d47fab8b552f849c3c48b.md)` shown as they were written (2026-11-14).

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-MSG-LINK-01 | 2026-11-14 | A model writes a markdown link to a file it delivered by its stored id, `[words](attachment:<id>)` (the core reads the same form, and also `attachment:attachments/<id>` and `attachments/<id>`). The message renderer never shows that target. When the id is one of the conversation's own files (what the owner attached to a turn, and what a member delivered in a media message of any turn of that conversation: `knownFiles`) the link is that file's chip (`fileChipHTML`, the chip of a file in the team folder) with the link's words, or the file's name when it has none, and pressing it opens that file by `data-act="attachment-open"` as every other file of a message opens (`attachments.path`, by id only). Any other target of the `attachment:` kind (an id no turn holds, a path, a drive letter, a malformed or upper-case id) is only its words, with no button, no link and no `data-id`. Only the stored-id form `^[a-f0-9]{32}\.[a-z0-9]{1,12}$` is ever read, so no path reaches `open_attachment`. A renderer that is not given the conversation's files (a group, search, copy) shows the words alone. A reply drawn before its file was known is drawn again once it is (the kept drawing and a turn's cache key carry the known ids its words mention). | vitest, look | hard | app/src/main/attachment-links.test.ts::only the stored ids of the two link forms are read; app/src/main/attachment-links.test.ts::a link to a file of the conversation is its chip, opened by its id; app/src/main/attachment-links.test.ts::a link to a file the conversation does not know is its words alone; app/src/main/attachment-links.test.ts::where nothing is known, the words stand alone and no raw target shows; app/src/main/attachment-links.test.ts::a routine's message links the file it delivered in the same turn; app/src/main/attachment-links.test.ts::a file of an earlier turn of the conversation opens from a later reply, and one of no turn does not; app/src/main/attachment-links.test.ts::a reply that was drawn before the file was known is drawn again once it is; app/src/main/pages/agent-attachment-link.test.ts::the link is the file's chip, which opens that file once; a file of no turn is its words alone; message-attachment-link |

## Skill learning follows Hermes Agent, and the data-floor refusal (2026-11-11)

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-SKILL-09 | 2026-11-11 | Owner, 2026-11-11: 「他現在一直建立 skills，我覺得可能不太好…參考一下 Hermes agents，然後把他們的邏輯行為拿過來」. A member's skill review follows Hermes Agent's nudge (agent/turn_finalizer.py, `skills.creation_nudge_interval`): it counts the steps of real work (tool, command, desktop; not reads of skills, memory, the time or the team, and not chat) since the last review or since `skill_manage` ran, per member, and is due once the count reaches `NUDGE_INTERVAL` = 10. It is decided at the end of a turn that finished normally and is the owner's: never for a routine's run, an exchange between members, or a turn that was stopped, failed or is still running. A review restarts the count when it starts, whatever comes of it. Every creation and update is logged at info level with the member, the skill name, created or updated and the source (review or tool), never content, so the log can be counted. | pytest | hard | core/tests/members/test_skill_growth.py::test_twelve_chat_turns_without_work_never_start_a_review; core/tests/members/test_skill_growth.py::test_review_counts_real_work_across_turns_uses_the_member_brain_and_logs; core/tests/members/test_skill_growth.py::test_skill_manage_restarts_the_count; core/tests/members/test_skill_growth.py::test_routine_exchange_and_interrupted_turns_never_start_a_review |
| R-SKILL-10 | 2026-11-11 | Owner, 2026-11-11 (the complaint was how often): limits Hermes does not have. At most one NEW learned skill per member in 24 hours (changes to a skill are not capped), at least six hours between two reviews of a member, at most three changes a pass, and a new skill whose name or description is near an existing one (plain word overlap on normalised names and descriptions, no model) becomes a change to that one; the tool refuses it with what to do instead. The review sees every skill's name and description and may update any of them (only documents are budgeted), and its instructions are Hermes's discipline in Raphael's words: nothing is saved unless the work clearly taught a reusable, non-trivial procedure or the owner corrected a way of working; patch the skill in use, then extend a skill of the same class, then create a class-level one; no setup failures, negative claims, transient errors or one-off narratives. ## A card says why it did not connect, and the owner's own Google (2026-11-11)

The owner's open task since 2026-10-25 (「接上 Composio 與自己的 Google 授權」): he tried to connect Gmail twice. Once the card said `not_configured` (no key then); once it ended in a bare 「無法連線」 although no Composio request had been sent, and the member could only say "press again".

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-CONNECT-01 | 2026-11-11 | Every way a connect attempt can fail is a named cause (`CAUSES` in `connectors/cards.py`), never the bare 「沒有連上」: one sentence in the window's language that says what happened and the one thing to do (check the key, sign in again, the service is down so try later, this PC is offline, the address is wrong, the service refused with its own short words), on the card with a retry only where pressing again can help (`retry`) and the place to put something right where it cannot (`fix`: the Composio key in Connections, or the card's own form). The attempt that used to be bare had failed before any request was sent: another sign-in or the connection list still held the connector (`busy`), the vault could not be read (`vault`), the browser could not be opened (`browser`). The member is told the same cause as data (`cause <name>: <what happened>`), so that it explains instead of saying "press again". What the code cannot name is `unknown`, said honestly, with its short technical reason (a word and a status, never JSON or a trace) folded under 詳細資料. A cause added in the core without words in the window fails a test. | pytest, vitest, look | hard | core/tests/test_connect_causes.py::test_a_connection_that_waits_for_another_is_named_busy_and_sends_nothing; core/tests/test_connect_causes.py::test_each_failure_of_the_service_is_a_named_cause_with_its_retry; core/tests/test_connect_causes.py::test_a_failure_the_code_cannot_name_says_so_and_keeps_the_short_reason; app/src/main/connector-card.test.ts::every cause the core can name has words in the window; app/src/main/connector-card.test.ts::what could not be named keeps its short technical reason folded under the details, never on the card's face; connector-cause-busy; connector-cause-refused; connector-cause-unknown |
| R-CONNECT-02 | 2026-11-11 | The owner's own Google is set up in the conversation, with the fewest buttons, and nowhere else (no settings page): when the owner asks a member to connect his own Google account, or picks 「Google（私人連接）」 in the store, a card (the app `google`) walks what to make in Google Cloud in five numbered steps, with the scopes and the return address as text to copy, takes the Client ID (plain) and the Client secret (masked), and 連接 Google saves both and signs in on the browser's loopback address with PKCE; the card then says which account connected and how many tools it opens, or the named cause. With a Client ID kept (a declined sign-in, an expired one) the card asks for nothing and one button signs in again; a client Google refused is asked for again. The row's 連結 (the store's 加入 before 2026-11-12) leaves for the composer with the owner's words, as n8n's does. | pytest, vitest, look | hard | core/tests/test_google_own.py::test_the_fields_become_a_signed_in_account_and_the_secret_is_kept_only_in_the_vault; core/tests/test_google_own.py::test_a_card_whose_client_is_kept_asks_only_for_the_sign_in; core/tests/test_google_own.py::test_a_client_google_refuses_is_asked_for_again_and_a_new_one_connects; core/tests/test_connect_causes.py::test_google_sign_in_failures_are_named; app/src/main/google-card.test.ts::walks the steps, gives the scopes and the address to copy, and asks for the two fields; app/src/main/google-card.test.ts::with a Client ID kept it asks for nothing: one button signs in, or signs in again; app/src/main/connections.test.ts::the view offers the owner's own Google, and its 連結 leaves for the conversation where the card walks the steps; connector-google-steps; connector-google-filled; connector-google-authorizing; connector-google-connected; connector-google-kept; connector-google-rejected |
| R-CONNECT-03 | 2026-11-11 | The Client secret (like every token and key) goes from its masked field to the core in the card's one call (`integrations.card_connect`) and into the vault through `connectors.google.configure`, and nowhere else: it is not in a card row, event, log, reply, model prompt, failure or setting file, it is not shown again, and a service's message that echoes it is redacted. ## A subscription CLI that cannot take a turn (2026-11-11)

Owner, 2026-11-11, with a screenshot of a member whose Claude Code sign-in had expired: the reply was one sentence for two causes (「還沒登入或缺少金鑰」) pointing at a settings page, with nothing to press, and he had to run `/login` in Claude himself.

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-CLI-SIGNIN-01 | 2026-11-11 | A subscription CLI that cannot take a turn says its true cause in one sentence that names the tool, in the window's language and in every interface language (`Failure.cause`, `_CAUSE_MESSAGES` in `failures.py`): its sign-in ran out (「Claude Code 的登入已過期。」, told by the CLI's refusal: a 401, an expired or unrefreshable token, Codex's refresh-token words, "Please run /login"), it never had one or was disconnected (「還沒登入。」, "Not logged in", or no credentials found before it starts), or it is not on this computer (「還沒安裝。」). Each is told apart from the others, from an API brain's key (a refused key is the provider's `開啟設定`, never "sign in") and from every other failure, which keeps its own kind and no cause. The sentence carries no code, no setting path, no raw output and no second cause (R-TEXT-01..04), and a refused session is not asked again with the same session. The cause is the turn's public failure (`cause`, only for `cli:` providers) and a room or routine run records it (`cli_login_expired`, `cli_login`, `cli_missing`, in the room's words 「CLI 的登入已過期」, 「需要登入 CLI」, 「CLI 還沒安裝」). | pytest | hard | core/tests/agent/test_cli_signin_cause.py::test_a_session_that_ran_out_is_expired; core/tests/agent/test_cli_signin_cause.py::test_a_cli_that_says_it_is_not_logged_in_is_signed_out; core/tests/agent/test_cli_signin_cause.py::test_any_other_failure_stays_what_it_was_and_has_no_cause; core/tests/agent/test_cli_signin_cause.py::test_the_sentence_names_the_tool_and_one_cause_in_every_language; core/tests/agent/test_cli_signin_cause.py::test_a_cause_reaches_the_turn_only_from_a_subscription_cli_and_without_the_reason; core/tests/members/test_cli_brains.py::test_an_expired_sign_in_is_said_as_expired_and_is_not_asked_again; core/tests/members/test_cli_brains.py::test_a_cli_that_is_not_installed_says_so_and_never_starts; core/tests/members/test_cli_brains.py::test_not_logged_in_never_starts_the_fake_cli; core/tests/background/test_room_cli.py::test_a_room_member_whose_cli_cannot_sign_in_reports_that_cause; core/tests/background/test_room_cli.py::test_a_routine_run_whose_cli_sign_in_ran_out_is_a_failed_run_of_that_cause; core/tests/agent/test_service.py::test_a_key_openrouter_refuses_is_still_auth_after_the_harness_attempt |
| R-CLI-SIGNIN-02 | 2026-11-11 | Under the failed reply of the newest answered turn there is ONE button that mends its cause where the owner is, the shared small button and sized to its words, with no paragraph: 「重新登入」 for a session that ran out, 「登入」 for none, the install guide the app already offers for a CLI that is not there. A sign-in that was never needed shows nothing, and an API model's refused key keeps its `開啟設定` only (R-UI-02). Pressing it starts that CLI's own sign-in the way Settings does (`brains.cli_login`, a console with `claude auth login`, `codex login` or Antigravity's own start), with `again` because a session that ran out still has its credentials; nothing reads or copies a credential file. While the window is open the reply says to finish there beside 「取消」; closing the window says 「沒有完成登入」 and keeps the button; cancelling or ten minutes give the button back; a second press while it is open starts no second console. When the sign-in finished (the process's own exit, and for Antigravity a login afterwards) the owner's question is sent again once by itself, only while that reply is still the newest answer and nothing else is under way, and the reply says 「已登入，正在重新送出」 meanwhile. | pytest, vitest, look | hard | core/tests/agent/test_cli_signin_cause.py::test_a_signed_in_login_that_the_owner_finishes_is_noticed_from_the_process; core/tests/agent/test_cli_signin_cause.py::test_a_console_the_owner_closes_is_a_stopped_sign_in_and_a_cancel_is_not; app/src/main/timeline.test.ts::the sentence is the core's and exactly one button follows it: 重新登入 for a session that ran out, 登入 for none; app/src/main/timeline.test.ts::a CLI that is not installed offers its install guide, for whichever CLI it is; app/src/main/timeline.test.ts::the window waiting for the sign-in says to finish there and offers 取消, a closed one says so and leaves the button; app/src/main/timeline.test.ts::only the newest answered reply carries it, and only a subscription CLI's own failure; app/src/main/cli-signin.test.ts::the button starts the CLI's own sign-in with `again`, waits while its window is open, and sends the question again once when it finished; app/src/main/cli-signin.test.ts::a window closed before the sign-in finished says so, leaves the button usable and sends nothing; app/src/main/cli-signin.test.ts::cancelling gives the button back at once and ends the console; app/src/main/cli-signin.test.ts::a window left open for ten minutes is ended and the button comes back; app/src/main/cli-brains-api.test.ts::a sign-in that ran out is started again, and the one this app started can be cancelled, through the same method; app/scripts/look/checks.mjs::chat-cli-signin (scenes chat-cli-expired, chat-cli-signed-out, chat-cli-not-installed, chat-cli-signin-waiting, chat-cli-signin-stopped, chat-cli-signin-done, chat-cli-signin-flow) |

## One row at the top of a conversation (2026-11-12)

Owner request of 2026-11-12 (「我不知道為什麼我們的UI 就是沒有像是grokbot 這麼clean 不管頁面大小都是 可以幫我不管排版 大小 文字都盡量達到他們的水準嗎」), after Grok Bot measured at the same sizes: it spends one row at the top of a conversation where Raphael spent two, and its thread passes under that row.

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-TOP-01 | 2026-11-12 | A conversation, a member's and a group's, has one row above its thread, and it is the window's own top row: the header's pill and its round buttons are centred 30 px from the window's top (Grok Bot's, measured), with no empty strip above them. The tools at its right end 14 px short of the window's three buttons where the pane reaches the window's edge; where another pane stands at the right (the details panel, a group's side thread) that pane's own head is the row over it, carries the window's buttons, and the conversation's tools go to their pane's edge. At narrow widths the sidebar is the rail of faces (R-SIDE-01), so the row starts at the pane's own edge: no menu button stands at the window's top left. The thread passes under the row: its first message keeps a top padding equal to the row, and what scrolls up fades out as it reaches the row (the thread's mask, a report's, nothing new), while the pill and the buttons stay on their own opaque surfaces. The row's empty parts still move the window and maximise it, and a wheel over the row still moves the thread; the pill and the buttons still press. The search bar, a page or a report open in the thread's place, and the conversation between members sit under or in place of the row without moving it. Every other page keeps its place under the window's strip. | vitest, look | hard | app/src/main/window-frame.test.ts::the row is 60 px, so the pill's centre stands 30 px from the window's top as Grok Bot's, and the buttons' cells are the width the tools stay clear of; app/src/main/window-frame.test.ts::the header takes no room from the thread, which has no strip above it and passes under the row; app/src/main/window-frame.test.ts::the tools end short of the window's buttons, and go to their pane's edge where the details panel or a side thread stands beside them; app/src/main/window-frame.test.ts::the row's empty parts move the window, and the strip's own drag cell steps aside over it so that the pill and the buttons press; app/src/main/conversation-header-drag.test.ts::the empty parts of a conversation's header move the window and a double press maximises it once, while its pill and buttons are not taken; app/src/main/conversation-scroll.test.ts::a wheel turned over the header moves the thread under it, or the report open in its place, and leaves a zoom alone; app/scripts/look/checks.mjs::conversation-top-row (scenes top-row-find, top-row-details, top-row-room, top-row-room-find, top-row-scrolled, top-row-wheel, chat, conversation-share, details, raphael-details, room, calm-thread, page-inplace, page-inplace-back, page-inplace-room, page-report, page-report-share, room-thread-open) |

## The accounts of an app, and a sign-in that waits (2026-11-13)

Owner, 2026-11-13, on the new build, with a picture of an app's page that listed 「Jack · 已連結」, 「帳號 2 · 等待登入」 and 「帳號 3 · 等待登入」: 「對了 帳號登入如果中途取消都要可以拿掉 不然他會有超級多沒有用的帳號卡在這裡」. And, with a picture of Connect Apps whose every 連結 button was grey: 「然後為什麼大部分人都沒有辦法連結？」.

| ID | Date | Rule | Check | Severity | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R-ACCT-01 | 2026-11-13 | A sign-in that was given up leaves no account, and every account row can be removed. The account Composio makes for a sign-in (INITIATED, INITIALIZING) is given back, in Composio and off the list, when the owner cancels the wait, when the flow ends without success (the page was closed, the browser could not open, the answer was none the core knows, or the ten minutes ran out) and whenever the accounts are read and it is older than ten minutes (Composio's created time, else the time Raphael began it; one an earlier build left with no time counts as old); the one being waited for now is never touched, an ACTIVE account is never given back this way, and a Composio that cannot be reached leaves the row for the next read. A row that Composio itself reported failed, expired or revoked stays, to be removed by hand. Each row of an app's accounts card is a person, a name, a status and the quiet ⋯ (no echo tooltip) with 重新命名 and then 移除 in red; there is no 帳號名稱 button. A row that is not connected is removed at once; a connected one asks the app's delete question (「移除「name」？」, 「成員將無法再使用這個帳號。」, 取消 and a red 移除) and is revoked in Composio first; the last connected account is the app, so its 移除 is the app's own two-press disconnect with its own words (core: `connectors.remove_account`, owner-only). A failure says so in the usual notice and leaves the row. | pytest, vitest, look | hard | core/tests/test_connector_accounts.py::test_a_waiting_sign_in_older_than_the_limit_is_given_back_when_the_accounts_are_read_from_composio; core/tests/test_connector_accounts.py::test_an_active_account_is_never_given_back_however_old; core/tests/test_connector_accounts.py::test_a_sign_in_nobody_finishes_ends_at_the_limit_and_leaves_no_account; core/tests/test_connector_accounts.py::test_removing_an_active_account_revokes_it_and_the_others_carry_on; core/tests/test_connector_accounts.py::test_the_last_active_account_is_the_app_and_removing_it_disconnects_the_app; app/src/main/connections.test.ts::the menu has 重新命名 and then 移除 in red, and renaming is what the 帳號名稱 button did; app/src/main/connections.test.ts::a connected account asks the app's delete question first, and only a yes removes it; accounts-card; accounts-menu; accounts-remove-ask; accounts-remove-yes; accounts-remove-waiting; accounts-remove-last; account-label-edit |
| R-ACCT-02 | 2026-11-13 | One sign-in waits at a time and it holds nothing back. While a sign-in waits for the browser every other app's 連結 stays pressable and the waiting row's 取消 is enabled whatever the sign-in is for (a managed Composio one included); 取消 ends the wait at the core, which gives back the account it made, and the row is 連結 again with nothing said. Pressing another app's 連結 ends the waiting one the same way and then starts the new one; pressing the waiting app's own button again starts nothing. A wait the owner walked away from ends at the ten minutes with a quiet line on that app's page (no toast, no red) and the row back to 連結. | vitest, pytest, look | hard | app/src/main/connections.test.ts::one sign-in waits at a time and holds nothing back: connecting another app ends it first, and a cancelled one says nothing; app/src/main/connections.test.ts::a sign-in that cannot be ended starts nothing on top of it; app/src/main/connections.test.ts::while a login is open the row says so with a button to cancel, and the other 連結 buttons stay pressable; core/tests/test_connector_accounts.py::test_a_sign_in_that_is_closed_or_fails_on_the_way_leaves_no_account; sign-in-waits; sign-in-replaced; sign-in-cancelled |
